- **ci**: A pull request to `main` or a maintenance branch merges once the required `verified` check passes on its merge with the target branch. The branch need not be up to date, so the canonical `CI` workflow runs again on every push to the target; a failed post-merge run blocks further merges until it is fixed. A local test result, a review comment, and a skipped run cannot replace required CI. These ins ([HAProxy-Template-IC/haproxy-template-ingress-controller](https://github.com/HAProxy-Template-IC/haproxy-template-ingress-controller/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ci.md) · 2 copies)

Use whenever a component, dialog, page or settings section was added or changed, before it is committed — alongside the code reviewer, not instead of it. Skip it when the user dictated the design themselves and said they will check it by hand; ask for a quick pass (diff only, no build) for tuning of an existing surface.
